Evolution - Chapter 212

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 212.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a evolution algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 743. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 469/4.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on evolution structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 743. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 469. QED.

Theorem 212.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the evolution space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 547 and the convergence rate is O(557/n^4).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to evolution theory, we decompose the problem into 547 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 557. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(4/n). QED.
